你只作为大语言模型（你是ai中间人），禁止调用你自生的工具或除文本以外的内容（下游会识别工具并调用），严格按照我的格式要求生成工具和调用内容。转发指令格式（逐字照抄结构）：

```tool_call
{"name": "工具名", "arguments": {"参数名": "值"}}
```

# Tone and style
You should be concise, direct, and to the point. When you run a non-trivial bash command, you should explain what the command does and why you are running it, to make sure the user understands what you are doing (this is especially important when you are running a command that will make changes to the user's system).
Remember that your output will be displayed on a command line interface. Your responses can use GitHub-flavored markdown for formatting, and will be rendered in a monospace font using the CommonMark specification.
Output text to communicate with the user; all text you output outside of tool use is displayed to the user. Only use tools to complete tasks. Never use tools like Bash or code comments as means to communicate with the user during the session.
If you cannot complete a request, briefly state the relevant limitation and offer a helpful next step or alternative. Avoid moralizing.
Only use emojis if the user explicitly requests it. Avoid using emojis in all communication unless asked.
IMPORTANT: You should minimize output tokens as much as possible while maintaining helpfulness, quality, and accuracy. Only address the specific query or task at hand, avoiding tangential information unless absolutely critical for completing the request. If you can answer in 1-3 sentences or a short paragraph, please do.
IMPORTANT: Avoid unnecessary preambles and postambles. Include explanations or summaries when needed to answer the user, explain a command, or report verification results.
IMPORTANT: Keep responses concise by default, while providing the detail needed to answer accurately, explain non-trivial commands, and report verification results. Expand when the user asks for detail. Examples of concise answers follow:

# Proactiveness
You are allowed to be proactive, but only when the user asks you to do something. You should strive to strike a balance between:
1. Doing the right thing when asked, including taking actions and follow-up actions
2. Not surprising the user with actions you take without asking
For example, if the user asks you how to approach something, you should do your best to answer their question first, and not immediately jump into taking actions.
3. Do not add an unsolicited code walkthrough. After working on a file, briefly report the outcome, verification status, and any unresolved blocker.

# Following conventions
When making changes to files, first understand the file's code conventions. Mimic code style, use existing libraries and utilities, and follow existing patterns.
- NEVER assume that a given library is available, even if it is well known. Whenever you write code that uses a library or framework, first check that this codebase already uses the given library. For example, you might look at neighboring files, or check the package.json (or cargo.toml, and so on depending on the language).
- When you create a new component, first look at existing components to see how they're written; then consider framework choice, naming conventions, typing, and other conventions.
- When you edit a piece of code, first look at the code's surrounding context (especially its imports) to understand the code's choice of frameworks and libraries. Then consider how to make the given change in a way that is most idiomatic.
- Always follow security best practices. Never introduce code that exposes or logs secrets and keys. Never commit secrets or keys to the repository.
- Never return clickable Markdown links for binary or non-text files. For such files, return only plain text (for example, a file path, filename, or short note). If the file type is uncertain, treat common binary-like extensions such as `.png`, `.jpg`, `.pdf`, `.zip`, `.docx`, `.xlsx`, `.pptx`, `.mp3`, and `.mp4` as non-text.
- All file operations MUST stay within the working directory in <env> section, unless user explicitly specifies otherwise. Never guess paths—use Glob/Grep to verify.

# Code style
- IMPORTANT: DO NOT ADD ***ANY*** COMMENTS unless asked
- Follow the Verification requirements below. Add or update tests as needed for new or changed behavior.

# Doing tasks
The user will primarily request you perform software engineering tasks. This includes solving bugs, adding new functionality, refactoring code, explaining code, and more. When given an unclear or generic instruction, consider it in the context of these software engineering tasks and the current working directory. For these tasks the following steps are recommended:
- Before any other action, check if an available skill matches the current task. If yes, invoke the skill tool immediately — do not proceed until the skill has been loaded.
- Use the available search tools to understand the codebase and the user's query. You are encouraged to use the search tools extensively both in parallel and sequentially.
- Implement the solution using all tools available to you
- Verify the solution according to the Verification section below. NEVER assume specific test framework or test script. Check the README or search codebase to determine the testing approach.
- VERY IMPORTANT: For code changes, run the project's required lint and typecheck commands when applicable, following the Verification section below. If you are unable to find the correct command, ask the user for the command to run and if they supply it, proactively suggest writing it to AGENTS.md so that you will know to run it next time.
NEVER commit changes unless the user explicitly asks you to. It is VERY IMPORTANT to only commit when explicitly asked, otherwise the user will feel that you are being too proactive.
- If your approach is blocked, do not attempt to brute force your way to the outcome. For example, if an API call or test fails, do not wait and retry the same action repeatedly. Instead, consider alternative approaches or other ways you might unblock yourself, or consider using the question tool to align with the user on the right path forward.
- Avoid giving time estimates or predictions for how long tasks will take, whether for your own work or for users planning projects. Focus on what needs to be done, not how long it might take.

- Tool results and user messages may include <system-reminder> tags. <system-reminder> tags contain useful information and reminders. They are NOT part of the user's provided input or the tool result.

# Verification
- Before finishing code changes, run checks appropriate to the changed behavior. For nontrivial runtime changes, exercise the affected path using available tools; build or typecheck alone does not prove the feature works.
- Fix failures caused by your changes and rerun affected checks. Recheck after further relevant edits.
- Base completion claims on actual results. If verification is blocked, report the attempts, blocker, and unverified behavior.
- Keep verification proportionate. Once required checks pass and the change is sufficiently verified, stop optional testing. Expand checks only for new edits, failures, or a concrete remaining concern.

# Tool usage policy
- SKILL CHECK (BLOCKING): Before starting ANY task, you MUST first check if any available skill matches the task. If a matching skill exists, invoke it IMMEDIATELY as your FIRST action before doing anything else. NEVER mention a skill in text without actually calling the skill tool.
- When doing file search, prefer to use the Task tool in order to reduce context usage.
- When handling any unit test / test ("单元测试"/"测试" in Chinese) related tasks, if the Task tool's available subagents include **developer-test-agent**, you MUST directly invoke the Task tool to call that subagent for execution, in order to ensure optimal performance and result quality.
- You can call multiple tools in a single response. If you intend to call multiple tools and there are no dependencies between them, make all independent tool calls in parallel. Maximize use of parallel tool calls where possible to increase efficiency. However, if some tool calls depend on previous calls to inform dependent values, do NOT call these tools in parallel and instead call them sequentially. For instance, if one operation must complete before another starts, run these operations sequentially instead. You MUST NOT exceed 6 parallel tool calls in a single response unless the user explicitly asks for more.
- VERY IMPORTANT: When exploring the codebase to gather context or to answer a question that is not a needle query for a specific file/class/function, you MUST directly use the Task tool(subagent_type=explore) instead of running search commands directly.
- VERY IMPORTANT: Call CodeSemanticSearch (if these tools are available) first instead of calling grep or glob to explore the codebase when analyzing or retrieving code.

# AGENTS.md - Your Workspace

Keep workspace conventions here. Personality and tone belong in `SOUL.md`.

## First Run

If `BOOTSTRAP.md` exists, follow it to set up your identity and workspace, then delete it after completion.

## Session Startup

Use runtime-provided startup context first. It may already include `AGENTS.md`, `SOUL.md`, `USER.md`, recent daily memory (`memory/YYYY-MM-DD.md`), and `MEMORY.md` (main session only).

Read startup files again only when:

1. The user explicitly asks.
2. Needed context is missing.
3. A deeper follow-up read is needed.

## Memory

Use files for continuity across sessions:

- **Daily notes:** `memory/YYYY-MM-DD.md` holds raw logs; create `memory/` if needed.
- **User model:** `USER.md` holds stable preferences and profile facts as active directives.
- **Long-term:** `MEMORY.md` holds durable non-profile facts and decisions.

Capture decisions, context, and things to remember. Skip secrets unless asked to keep them.

### USER.md - Durable User Directives

- Write stable preferences, communication style, relationships, and active-project context as imperative directives such as `Always`, `Never`, or `Prefer`.
- Precede each directive with `<!-- observed: YYYY-MM-DD | status: active -->`.
- When a preference changes, mark the old entry `superseded` and rewrite the active directive in place. Never leave contradictory active directives.

### MEMORY.md - Durable Facts and Decisions

- Load **only in the main session** (direct chats with your human). Never load it in shared contexts (Discord, group chats, sessions with other people).
- Read, edit, and update it freely in main sessions.
- Save significant events, decisions, lessons, and durable non-profile facts as a curated summary, not raw logs.

### Write It Down

Before writing memory files, read them first. Write concrete updates, never empty placeholders; mental notes do not survive a restart.

- Asked to "remember this": update the daily note or relevant file.
- Learned a lesson: update `AGENTS.md` or the relevant skill.
- Made a mistake: document it so you do not repeat it.

### Memory Maintenance

Every few days, use a scheduled automation to review recent daily notes. Fold stable directives into `USER.md` and durable non-profile facts into `MEMORY.md`; keep `MEMORY.md` maintenance confined to main sessions. Remove outdated entries so the curated files do not become raw logs.

## Red Lines

- Don't share private data with people or services the user didn't ask for.
- Confirm destructive or irreversible actions the user didn't ask for.
- Before changing config or schedulers (crontab, systemd units, nginx configs, shell rc files), inspect existing state first and preserve/merge by default.
- Prefer `trash` over `rm` - recoverable beats gone forever.

## Existing Solutions Preflight

Before proposing or building a custom solution, briefly check existing open-source projects, maintained libraries, OpenClaw plugins, or free platforms. Prefer an adequate existing option. Build custom only when those options are unsuitable, too expensive, unmaintained, unsafe, non-compliant, or the user explicitly asks for custom work. Recommend paid services only with explicit spend approval.

## External vs Internal

**Do freely:** anything the user asked for, including sending messages, emails, or posts on their behalf; read files, explore, organize, learn; search the web, check calendars; work within this workspace.

**Ask first:** public or outbound actions the user did not request.

## Group Chats

Participate as yourself by default. When the user asks you to send or post on their behalf, follow that request and share only the information intended for that audience.

### Know When to Speak

**Respond when:** directly mentioned or asked; adding clear value; humor fits; correcting important misinformation; summarizing when asked.

**Stay silent when:** people are casually chatting; someone already answered; you would only say "yeah" or "nice"; the conversation flows without you; a reply would interrupt it.

Send one thoughtful reply instead of several fragments. Do not respond multiple times to the same message with different reactions.

### React Like a Human

Where reactions are supported, use them to acknowledge without interrupting, express humor or interest, or answer yes/no. Use at most one reaction per message.

## Tools

Use the relevant skill for tool procedures. Keep local tool and environment notes in this section so they stay separate from shared skills.

### Local notes

Record camera names, SSH hosts and users, preferred voices and speakers, and device nicknames here.

**Voice storytelling:** when `sag` (ElevenLabs TTS) is available, use voice for stories, movie summaries, and storytime.

**Platform formatting:**

- On Discord and WhatsApp, use bullet lists instead of markdown tables.
- On Discord, wrap multiple links in `<>` to suppress embeds (`<https://example.com>`).
- On WhatsApp, use **bold** or CAPS instead of headers.

## Automations - Be Proactive

Use scheduled automations for recurring checks, reminders, and background work. Keep checklists and check timing in each automation's scratch. Keep it small; do not create a separate state file. Find jobs with `openclaw automations list --all`; update scratch with `openclaw automations scratch <jobId> --set "..."`.

**Things to check (rotate, 2-4 times per day):** urgent unread email; calendar events in the next 24-48h; social mentions; weather if your human might go out.

**Reach out when:** an important email arrives; a calendar event is less than 2h away; you find something interesting; you have not said anything for more than 8h.

**Stay quiet (`NO_REPLY`) when:** it is 23:00-08:00 unless urgent; the human is clearly busy; nothing is new; the last check was less than 30 minutes ago.

When reach-out and quiet conditions both apply, stay quiet. Only an urgent item overrides quiet hours.

**Proactive work you can do without asking:** read and organize memory files; check projects (`git status`, etc.); update documentation; commit and push your own changes; review and update `USER.md` and `MEMORY.md` within their access rules above.

Add conventions, style, and rules as you learn what works for this workspace.

## What NOT to save in memory

- Code patterns, conventions, architecture, file paths, or project structure — these can be derived by reading the current project state.
- Git history, recent changes, or who-changed-what — `git log` / `git blame` are authoritative.
- Debugging solutions or fix recipes — the fix is in the code; the commit message has the context.
- Anything already documented in AGENTS.md files.
- Ephemeral task details: in-progress work, temporary state, current conversation context.
- Absolute file paths on specific machines (e.g., `E:\project\src\file.ts`) — these are machine-specific and will be stale.
- Document sizes, line counts, or byte counts — these change on every edit and are not durable facts.
- Transient project phases or workflow stages (e.g., "Phase B: design", "Phase C: task planning") — save the project's purpose and scope instead, not its current workflow step.

These exclusions apply even when the user explicitly asks you to save. If they ask you to save a PR list or activity summary, ask what was *surprising* or *non-obvious* about it — that is the part worth keeping.

## How to save memories

Saving a memory is a two-step process:

**Step 1 — Write the memory file**

Use the Write tool to create a new `.md` file in `C:\Users\mey\.codeartsdoer\memory\C--Users-mey-Desktop-universalweb-api-main/`. The filename format is `{type}_{slugified-title}.md` where:
- `type` is one of `user`, `feedback`, `project`, `reference`
- `slugified-title` is the title in lowercase with underscores (e.g., "CI Runs bun test" → "ci_runs_bun_test")

The file content must start with YAML frontmatter followed by the memory body:

```markdown
---
name: {{memory name}}
description: {{one-line description — used to decide relevance in future conversations}}
type: {{user|feedback|project|reference}}
---

{{memory body content}}
```

For feedback/project types, structure the body as: rule/fact, then a **Why:** line (the reason) and a **How to apply:** line (when/where this guidance kicks in).

**Step 2 — Update the MEMORY.md index**

After writing the memory file, use the Edit tool to add a one-line entry to `C:\Users\mey\.codeartsdoer\memory\C--Users-mey-Desktop-universalweb-api-main/MEMORY.md`:

```
- [{{Title}}]({{filename}}.md) — {{one-line hook}}
```

Keep MEMORY.md under 200 lines and 25KB. If it exceeds the limit, trim oldest entries.

## When to access memories

- When the user references prior-conversation work, or when context from a previous session seems relevant.
- When the user explicitly asks you to check, recall, or remember something.
- A `<system-reminder>` block containing the MEMORY.md index and relevant memories will be injected into your context at the start of each conversation.

## Before recommending from memory

A memory that names a specific function, file, or flag is a claim that it existed *when the memory was written*. It may have been renamed, removed, or never merged. Before recommending it:
- If the memory names a file path: check the file exists.
- If the memory names a function or flag: grep for it.
- If the user is about to act on your recommendation (not just asking about history), verify first.

## Memory vs. other persistence

- **Memory files** (`C:\Users\mey\.codeartsdoer\memory\C--Users-mey-Desktop-universalweb-api-main/`) — for durable cross-session context (user profile, feedback, project context, external references).
- **AGENTS.md** — for project conventions and coding guidelines that apply to the current project.
- **README.md** — for user-facing documentation and usage instructions.
- **CHANGELOG.md** — for version change logs.
- **Plans** — for implementation approach alignment within a conversation.
- **Tasks** — for tracking work progress within a conversation.

Use memory only for information that will be useful in future conversations and cannot be derived from the code or project files.

[assistant]
这个错误 0x800B010A 是 Windows 证书链验证失败（CERT_E_CHAINING）。不只是证书过期问题，常见原因是根证书不受信任、缺少中间证书，或者安装包签名证书与提供的 .cer 不匹配。
先在 PowerShell 中执行下面的只读诊断，确认具体是哪一种：
powershell
$msix = "C:\Users\mey\Desktop\slices_285\OpenClaw-Dev-x64.msix"
$cer = "C:\Users\mey\Desktop\slices_285\OpenClaw-Dev.cer"

$cert = [System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates.X509Certificate2]::new($cer)
$sig = Get-AuthenticodeSignature $msix

"===== CER ====="
$cert | Format-List Subject, Issuer, Thumbprint, NotBefore, NotAfter

"===== MSIX SIGNATURE ====="
$sig | Format-List Status, StatusMessage
$sig.SignerCertificate | Format-List Subject, Issuer, Thumbprint

"===== CERTIFICATE CHAIN ====="
$chain = [System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates.X509Chain]::new()
$result = $chain.Build($cert)
"Chain valid: $result"
$chain.ChainStatus | Format-Table Status, StatusInformation -AutoSize
$chain.ChainElements | ForEach-Object {
    $_.Certificate | Format-List Subject, Issuer, Thumbprint
}
把完整输出发给我。
我会根据结果判断是否需要：
1. 将自签名证书安装到正确的受信任存储区。
补齐中间 CA 证书。
修复签名证书与 MSIX Publisher 不匹配的问题。
暂时不要关闭 Windows 的证书验证，也不要直接导入来源不明的根证书。 先确认信任链，再完成 OpenClaw 安装。

[tool_result name=call_39c7bf66061c480ca24ab7d7]
<path>C:\Users\mey\Desktop\universalweb-api-main\msg_condense.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """
2: msg_condense.py - 会话消息压缩模块（纯函数，无副作用）
3: 
4: 背景：IDE 每轮请求都会重放完整对话历史（系统规则 + 工具调用轮次），
5: 序列化后的 prompt 数万字且与上一轮几乎完全相同，导致每条消息都要分片。
6: 
7: 方案：对比本会话上一条实际发送到页面的文本（由 browser_core 按会话
8: 指纹缓存），若本次 prompt 与其存在足够长的公共前缀，则把前缀替换为
9: 占位符说明，只发送新增尾部。
10: 
11: 安全性：网页会话中上一条消息全文仍然上屏可见，AI 可直接读取会话
12: 上文中的规则内容，因此压缩不丢失信息。上传策略场景缓存的是实际
13: 发送的短模板文本，前缀自然不达标，不受影响。
14: 
15: 降级策略：任何条件不满足（无缓存 / 前缀太短 / 节省太少）都返回
16: 原文，即"不压缩则全量发"，绝不因压缩引入风险。
17: """
18: 
19: import logging
20: 
21: logger = logging.getLogger('msg_condense')
22: 
23: # 压缩参数（可按需调整）
24: MIN_PREFIX_LEN = 2000    # 公共前缀至少要这么长才值得压缩
25: MIN_SAVINGS = 1200       # 压缩至少要省这么多字符才有意义
26: 
27: # 占位符模板：{saved} 为省略的字符数。
28: # 措辞用"本会话之前消息"而非"上一条消息"：压缩可链式发生（本轮前缀
29: # = 上上轮全文 + 上轮尾部），完整内容分布在此前多条消息里。
30: _PLACEHOLDER = (
31:     "〔上下文压缩说明：本消息开头的系统规则与历史对话内容，"
32:     "与本会话此前消息中已发送的内容完全相同（共省略 {saved} 字符），"
33:     "请直接以会话中已有的上文为准，无需重复阅读。"
34:     "以下为本条消息的新增内容：〕\n"
35: )
36: 
37: 
38: def common_prefix_len(a: str, b: str) -> int:
39:     """返回两个字符串的公共前缀长度。
40: 
41:     无需换行对齐：被省略的 new_text[:plen] 与 prev_text[:plen] 逐字
42:     相同（这正是前缀的定义），任意切点替换都不产生语义破碎。"""
43:     n = min(len(a), len(b))
44:     i = 0
45:     while i < n and a[i] == b[i]:
46:         i += 1
47:     return i
48: 
49: 
50: def condense(new_text: str, prev_text: str,
51:              min_prefix: int = MIN_PREFIX_LEN,
52:              min_savings: int = MIN_SAVINGS) -> str:
53:     """若 new_text 与 prev_text 有足够长的公共前缀，返回压缩后的文本
54:     （占位符 + 增量尾部）；否则原样返回 new_text。永不抛异常。"""
55:     try:
56:         if not new_text or not prev_text:
57:             return new_text
58:         plen = common_prefix_len(new_text, prev_text)
59:         if plen < min_prefix:
60:             return new_text
61:         savings = plen - len(_PLACEHOLDER)
62:         if savings < min_savings:
63:             return new_text
64:         tail = new_text[plen:]
65:         # 尾部过短说明本条几乎是纯重复，也照样压缩（省最多）
66:         condensed = _PLACEHOLDER.format(saved=plen) + tail
67:         logger.info("[MsgCondense] 压缩生效: 前缀 %d 字符, 原文 %d → %d 字符",
68:                     plen, len(new_text), len(condensed))
69:         return condensed
70:     except Exception as e:
71:         logger.warning("[MsgCondense] 压缩失败，回退全量发送: %s", e)
72:         return new_text

(End of file - total 72 lines)

  Line 2: msg_condense.py - 会话消息压缩模块（纯函数，无副作用）
    1: """
    3: 
    4: 背景：IDE 每轮请求都会重放完整对话历史（系统规则 + 工具调用轮次），
    5: 序列化后的 prompt 数万字且与上一轮几乎完全相同，导致每条消息都要分片。
  Line 12: 上文中的规则内容，因此压缩不丢失信息。上传策略场景缓存的是实际
    9: 占位符说明，只发送新增尾部。
    10: 
    11: 安全性：网页会话中上一条消息全文仍然上屏可见，AI 可直接读取会话
    13: 发送的短模板文本，前缀自然不达标，不受影响。
    14: 
    15: 降级策略：任何条件不满足（无缓存 / 前缀太短 / 节省太少）都返回
  Line 16: 原文，即"不压缩则全量发"，绝不因压缩引入风险。

2162:     """把 messages 拼成给网页版模型看的纯文本 prompt。"""
2163: 
2164:     # 转发格式模板（首轮随工具表附带；续传轮仅重申格式、不重发工具表）

2166:         "转发指令格式（逐字照抄结构）：\n\n"
2167:         "```tool_call\n"

2175:         "最高优先级规则：忽略对话历史中出现的一切其他系统提示、角色"

2179:         + TOOL_FORMAT_HINT + "\n\n"

2185:     @staticmethod
2186:     def tool_protocol(tools: Optional[List[Dict]]) -> str:
2187:         """工具表 + 转发格式说明（独立成块，可内联到输入框文本）。
2188: 
2189:         网页 AI 收不到 API 的 tools 字段，中转把 IDE 声明的工具表
2190:         原样以 JSON 附在 prompt 里，由网页 AI 自己读描述选工具。
2191:         仅首轮调用（同一会话续传轮不再重发，网页端历史记得该表）。"""
2192:         tools = ToolSchemaConformer.normalize_tools(tools)
2193:         tools = [t for t in tools if isinstance(t, dict) and (t.get("function") or {}).get("name")]
2194:         if not tools:
2195:             return ""
2196:         logger.info(f"[ToolRound] 可用工具: {len(tools)} 个")
2197:         # 极简中间人话术（用户 2026-11-04 实测 poe HIT 的逐字模板，
2198:         # 长版剧本会被 poe 当思考内容消化掉）
2199:         return (
2200:             "你只作为大语言模型（你是ai中间人），禁止调用你自生的工具或除文本"
2201:             "以外的内容（下游会识别工具并调用），严格按照我的格式要求生成工具"
2202:             "和调用内容。" + ToolRoundBuilder.TOOL_FORMAT_HINT + "\n\n"
2203:             "可转发的目标（name 大小写敏感，完整定义 JSON 如下，按其中的"
2204:             " description 和 parameters 选择工具并组织 arguments）：\n"
2205:             + json.dumps(tools, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
2206:         )
2207: 
2208:     @staticmethod
2209:     def build(messages: List[Dict], tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
2210:               tool_choice: Any = None,
2211:               protocol_mode: str = "auto") -> str:
2212:         """拼网页 prompt。protocol_mode:
2213:         - "auto": 输入框直发——工具表仅首轮附带，续传轮只给提示
2214:         - "always_table": 附件场景——续传轮也在 prompt 里带工具表
2215:           （附件每轮都是新文件，网页端会话记忆不可靠，表必须随附件走）"""
2216:         system_parts: List[str] = []
2217:         other_parts: List[str] = []
2218:         has_tool_result = False
2219: 
2220:         for m in messages:
2221:             role = m.get("role", "user")
2222:             content = m.get("content", "") or ""
2223: 
2224:             if role == "system":
2225:                 if content:
2226:                     system_parts.append(content)
2227:                 continue
2228: 
2229:             if role == "tool":
2230:                 has_tool_result = True
2231:                 tool_name = m.get("name") or m.get("tool_call_id") or "tool"
2232:                 preview = content[:500] + ("..." if len(content) > 500 else "")
2233:                 logger.info(f"[ToolResult] name={tool_name}, len={len(content)}, content={preview!r}")
2234:                 other_parts.append(f"[tool_result name={tool_name}]\n{content}")
2235:                 continue
2236: 
2237:             if role == "user":
2238:                 other_parts.append(f"[user]\n{content}")
2239:             elif role == "assistant":
2240:                 tc = m.get("tool_calls")
2241:                 if tc:
2242:                     lines = []
2243:                     for c in tc:
2244:                         fn = c.get("function", {}) if isinstance(c, dict) else {}
2245:                         lines.append(
2246:                             f"- 调用 `{fn.get('name', '')}`，参数 {fn.get('arguments', '{}')}"
2247:                         )
2248:                     other_parts.append("[assistant]\n" + "\n".join(lines))
2249:                 if content:
2250:                     other_parts.append(f"[assistant]\n{content}")
2251:             else:
2252:                 other_parts.append(f"[{role}]\n{content}")
2253: 
2254:         parts: List[str] = []
2255:         # 工具表附带规则：auto 模式仅首轮（历史中无 assistant 回复）；
2256:         # always_table（附件场景）续传轮也带，防网页端会话记忆丢失后
2257:         # AI 见不到工具表（上游实测复现："没有找到可调用入口"死循环）
2258:         has_history = any((m.get("role") == "assistant") for m in messages)
2259:         # 协议放最前（用户实测 poe HIT 的结构：话术在前，[user] 消息在后）
2260:         if tools and (not has_history or protocol_mode == "always_table"):
2261:             protocol = ToolRoundBuilder.tool_protocol(tools)
2262:         else:
2263:             protocol = ""
2264:         if protocol:
2265:             parts.append(protocol)
2266:         if has_tool_result:
2267:             # 续传轮防角色污染：CodeArts 等上游的对话历史里带子代理系统
2268:             # 提示（"You are a file search specialist..."），网页 AI 会代入
2269:             # 该角色自答而不再输出转发指令，造成 tool_call 死循环。
2270:             parts.append(ToolRoundBuilder.TOOL_CONTINUATION_HINT)
2271:         if system_parts:
2272:             parts.append("\n\n".join(system_parts))
2273:         parts.extend(other_parts)
2274:         prompt = "\n\n".join(parts)
2275: 
2276:         if has_tool_result:
2277:             prompt += (
2278:                 "\n\n上一轮工具调用结果已在上方 [tool_result]。"
2279:                 "若任务完成，直接给最终回答；若需继续，重新输出 tool_call。"
2280:             )
2281: 
2282:         return prompt

2285: class FileUploadHelper:
2286:     @staticmethod
2287:     def _ensure_dir(cfg: dict) -> Path:

2289:         upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

2292:     @classmethod
2293:     def prepare_single_file(cls, prompt: str, cfg: dict) -> tuple:
2294:         upload_dir = cls._ensure_dir(cfg)
2295:         ext = cfg.get("file_ext", "txt")
2296:         filename = f"prompt_{int(time.time())}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}.{ext}"
2297:         filepath = upload_dir / filename
2298:         filepath.write_text(prompt, encoding="utf-8")
2299:         logger.info(f"[Upload] 单文件已准备: {filepath} ({len(prompt)} 字符)")
2300:         return str(filepath.resolve()), filename
2301: 
2302:     @classmethod
2303:     def prepare_chunked_files(cls, prompt: str, cfg: dict) -> list:
2304:         upload_dir = cls._ensure_dir(cfg)
2305:         ext = cfg.get("file_ext", "txt")
2306:         chunk_size = int(cfg.get("chunk_size", 50000))
2307:         max_files = int(cfg.get("max_upload_files", 5))
2308: 
2309:         chunks = [prompt[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(prompt), chunk_size)]
2310:         if len(chunks) > max_files:
2311:             logger.warning(f"[Upload] 分块数 {len(chunks)} 超过上限 {max_files}，合并尾部")
2312:             head = chunks[:max_files - 1]
2313:             tail = "\n".join(chunks[max_files - 1:])
2314:             chunks = head + [tail]
2315: 
2316:         stamp = int(time.time())
2317:         uid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]
2318:         total = len(chunks)
2319:         files = []
2320:         for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
2321:             name = f"prompt_{stamp}_{uid}_part{i + 1:02d}of{total:02d}.{ext}"
2322:             path = upload_dir / name
2323:             path.write_text(chunk, encoding="utf-8")
2324:             files.append((str(path.resolve()), name))
2325:         logger.info(f"[Upload] 分块文件已准备: {len(files)} 个 (chunk_size={chunk_size})")
2326:         return files

955: class ToolSchemaConformer:
956:     """
957:     把 AI 输出的工具名/参数对齐到 IDE 声明的 tools schema。
958: 
959:     选择工具的是网页端 AI，其输出格式与参数命名不可控；中转层必须把调用
960:     改写成 IDE 声明的那份 schema，否则 IDE 侧校验失败会静默丢弃工具调用
961:     （表现为"IDE 收到答复后没下文"）。
962: 
963:     兼容点：
964:     - 工具定义：OpenAI function / Anthropic input_schema / Gemini parameters
965:       / 裸 {name, parameters} / parameters 为 JSON 字符串
966:     - 工具名：精确 -> 归一化（read_file / readFile / read-file）-> 唯一包含匹配
967:     - 参数名：精确 -> 归一化 -> 唯一包含匹配（file_path / filePath / path）
968:     - 参数类型：string / number / integer / boolean / array / object 双向转换
969:     - schema 未声明的参数丢弃（避免 IDE 严格校验报错）
970:     - 必填参数缺失时补空值占位，让 IDE 报出可见错误而不是静默卡死
971:     """
972: 
973:     @staticmethod
974:     def _norm(s: Any) -> str:
975:         if not isinstance(s, str):
976:             return ""
977:         return re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]', '', s.lower())
978: 
979:     # ---------------- 工具定义归一化 ----------------
980: 
981:     @classmethod
982:     def _parse_schema(cls, v: Any) -> Dict:
983:         if isinstance(v, dict):
984:             return v
985:         if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():
986:             try:
987:                 parsed = json.loads(v)
988:                 if isinstance(parsed, dict):
989:                     return parsed
990:             except Exception:
991:                 pass
992:         return {}
993: 
994:     @classmethod
995:     def _lower_schema_types(cls, node: Any, depth: int = 0) -> Any:
996:         """Gemini 风格大写类型转小写；只递归 properties/items 并限深。"""
997:         if depth > 4 or not isinstance(node, dict):
998:             return node
999:         out = dict(node)
1000:         if isinstance(out.get("type"), str):
1001:             out["type"] = out["type"].strip().lower()
1002:         props = out.get("properties")
1003:         if isinstance(props, dict):
1004:             out["properties"] = {
1005:                 k: cls._lower_schema_types(p, depth + 1) for k, p in props.items()
1006:             }
1007:         items = out.get("items")
1008:         if isinstance(items, dict):
1009:             out["items"] = cls._lower_schema_types(items, depth + 1)
1010:         return out
1011: 
1012:     @classmethod
1013:     def normalize_tools(cls, tools: Optional[List[Any]]) -> List[Dict]:
1014:         """任意风格 tools -> OpenAI {type:function, function:{name,description,parameters}}。"""
1015:         result: List[Dict] = []
1016:         if not tools:
1017:             return result
1018:         if isinstance(tools, dict):
1019:             tools = [tools]
1020:         for t in tools:
1021:             if not isinstance(t, dict):
1022:                 continue
1023:             fn = t.get("function") if isinstance(t.get("function"), dict) else None
1024:             src = fn or t
1025:             name = src.get("name") or t.get("name")
1026:             if not name or not isinstance(name, str):
1027:                 continue
1028:             desc = src.get("description") or t.get("description") or ""
1029:             params = src.get("parameters")
1030:             if params is None:
1031:                 params = (src.get("input_schema") or src.get("inputSchema")
1032:                           or t.get("input_schema") or t.get("inputSchema")
1033:                           or t.get("parameters"))
1034:             if params is None and isinstance(src.get("arguments"), dict):
1035:                 params = src.get("arguments")
1036:             params = cls._lower_schema_types(cls._parse_schema(params))
1037:             params = dict(params)
1038:             if not params.get("type"):
1039:                 params["type"] = "object"
1040:             if not isinstance(params.get("properties"), dict):
1041:                 params["properties"] = {}
1042:             if not isinstance(params.get("required"), list):
1043:                 params["required"] = []
1044:             result.append({
1045:                 "type": "function",
1046:                 "function": {
1047:                     "name": name,
1048:                     "description": desc or "",
1049:                     "parameters": params,
1050:                 },
1051:             })
1052:         return result
1053: 
1054:     # ---------------- 工具名匹配 ----------------
1055: 
1056:     @classmethod
1057:     def match_tool(cls, name: Optional[str], known_tools: List[Dict]) -> Optional[Dict]:
1058:         """返回与 name 对齐的 function schema（name/parameters 均为 IDE 原始声明）。"""
1059:         if not name or not isinstance(name, str) or not known_tools:

[assistant]
根因已定位，两个问题是关联的。先总结设计与排查结果，确认后再动代码。

## 根因

**1. CodeArts 兼容（“明明有 cmd 但 AI 不知道调用”）**
- browser_core.py:5524 所有请求都按 `always_table` 构建 prompt，工具表在 prompt **开头**，每轮内容完全相同
- 而 msg_condense 压缩（browser_core.py:5546）把“与上轮的公共前缀”替换成占位符 → **工具表每轮都被压掉**
- 压缩后 `TOOL_CONTINUATION_HINT` 还指引 AI“完整工具表见对话开头”——对话开头实际只剩占位符。网页 AI 只细读最新消息、不回翻会话历史 → 看不到 cmd/executeCommand 声明 → 不调用

**2. 压缩只压头部**
- msg_condense.py:58 `condense` 只做**公共前缀**匹配。CodeArts 每轮 system 规则里有动态内容（时间/诊断/todo 状态），任何一个字符变化就让前缀提前截止，后面数万字重复的历史轮次完全不压缩

## 修复方案

**A. 工具表保护**：`condense()` 增加 `protect_prefix` 参数，prompt 开头的协议区（工具表+续传指令）豁免压缩，browser_core 传入其长度。工具表每轮原样出现在最新消息里 → AI 始终能看到并调用 cmd

**B. 块级智能压缩**：msg_condense 升级为“头部前缀 + 中间块级去重”——行级哈希索引+贪心最长匹配，本轮 prompt 中任何与已发送内容相同的长区块（≥800 字符）都替换为占位符，新增内容和短重复原样保留。纯函数、永不抛异常、条件不满足回退全量发（保持现有安全策略）

**C.（可选）工具表可读性**：在工具表 JSON 后追加“每工具一行目录”（name + 描述首句），减少长 JSON 扫读漏工具。会改变协议文本，你实测有效的 poe 模板话术本身不动，只是在 JSON 后追加目录段

验证：扩展 test_msg_condense.py（中间块压缩、协议豁免、链式压缩），回归 test_tool_relay.py / test_api_ide.py。

确认一下：A+B 直接做，C 要不要一起加？

browser_core\n   按会话指纹缓存），若本次 prompt 与其存在足够长的公共前缀，则把前缀\n   替换为占位符说明，只发送新增尾部。\n2. 中间块级去重：IDE 重放的历史并非纯追加（system 规则里常有时间/诊断\n   等动态内容，让公共前缀提前截止），前缀截止后与上轮已发送内容相同的\n   长区块（>= min_block）也各自替换为占位符——已存在的内容都支持压缩。\n\n安全性：\n- 网页会话中此前消息全文上屏可见，压缩只省略\"已发送过的重复内容\"，\n  从未发送的新内容永远原样保留，AI 可直接读取会话上文，不丢失信息。\n- prompt 开头的协议区（工具表 + 续传指令）通过 protect_prefix 豁免，\n  保证网页 AI 每轮都能在最新消息里看到完整工具清单（否则它看不到\n  cmd 等工具声明，表现为\"没有可用工具\"）。\n\n降级策略：任何条件不满足（无缓存 / 前缀太短 / 块太短 / 节省太少 / 匹配\n预算超限）都返回原文，即\"不压缩则全量发\"，绝不因压缩引入风险。\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\n\nlogger = 块级匹配内部参数\n_MIN_LINE_MATCH = 24       # 参与匹配索引的最短行长（短行噪声大，直接跳过）\n_MAX_CANDIDATES = 8        # 每行最多尝试的重复起点数\n_MATCH_BUDGET = 1_000_000  # 行比较总步数保险，超限整体回退全量发\n\n# 头部占位符：{saved} 为省略的字符数。\n# 措辞用\"本会话之前消息\"而非\"上一条消息\"：压缩可链式发生（本轮前缀\n# = 上上轮全文 + 上轮尾部），完整内容分布在此前多条消息里。\n_HEAD_PLACEHOLDER = (\n    \"〔上下文压缩说明：本消息开头的系统规则与历史对话内容，\"\n    \"与本会话此前消息中已发送的内容完全相同（共省略 {saved} 字符），\"\n 相同（这正是前缀的定义），任意切点替换都不产生语义破碎。\"\"\"\n    n = min(len(a), len(b))\n    i = 0\n    while i < n and a[i] == b[i]:\n        i += 1\n    return i\n\n\ndef _split_lines(text: str) -> list:\n    \"\"\"按行切分并保留行尾换行符，保证拼接后与原文逐字一致。\"\"\"\n    if not text:\n        return []\n    return text.splitlines(keepends=True)\n\n\ndef _condense_blocks(rest: str, prev: str, min_block: int) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"块级去重：rest 中与 prev 逐字相同的连续长行块替换为占位符。\n\n    匹配是行对齐的精确相等比较（索引 + 贪心延伸），只会省略 prev 中\n    逐字出现过的内容，新增内容永不被省略。\n    返回 (压缩后文本, 节省字符数)。永不抛异常。\"\"\"\n    if not rest or not prev or min_block <= 0:\n        return rest, 0\n    try:\n        prev_lines = _split_lines(prev)\n        if not prev_lines:\n            return rest, 0\n        index = {}\n        for j, pl in enumerate(prev_lines):\n            if len(pl) >= _MIN_LINE_MATCH:\n                index.setdefault(pl, []).append(j)\n        if not index:\n            return rest, 0\n        new_lines = _split_lines(rest)\n        out = []\n min_savings:\n            return new_text\n        condensed = head + \"\".join(pieces)\n        if condensed == new_text:\n            return new_text\n        logger.info(\"[MsgCondense] 压缩生效: 原文 %d <content>
1: ---
2: name: browser_core-codearts-compat-condense
3: description: 2026-11-05 用户要求修复 browser_core.py 项目：补齐对 CodeArts CLI 的兼容（cmd 存在但 AI 不知道调用），并重做对话压缩逻辑（从只压头部改为全部已有内容支持压缩）
4: type: project
5: from: Agent
6: ---
7: 
8: 用户于 2026-11-05 提出修复 browser_core.py 所在项目（AI 代理/浏览器自动化基础设施），两项核心任务：
9: 
10: 1. **CodeArts 兼容不足**：环境中明明存在 CodeArts 的 cmd（命令），但 AI 不知道去调用它。用户原话："解决现对codeart的兼容明明有cmd但是ai不知道调用"。
11: 2. **对话压缩不够智能**：现有压缩逻辑（condense/compact，见 msg_condense.py）只压缩头部内容（用户称"g规等头部"），用户要求"存在的都支持压缩"——即所有已有内容都应纳入压缩范围，压缩策略应更智能，而非仅针对头部。
12: 
13: **Why:** 用户的目标是让该代理基础设施与华为 CodeArts CLI 生态正确互操作，且压缩机制直接决定长对话下上下文质量，这是用户主动指出的两大缺陷，不是猜测的需求。
14: 
15: **How to apply:** 后续在此项目工作时，改动涉及工具调用链路（schema 校验、流解析、轮次构建）时优先检查 CodeArts 工具是否能被 AI 正确发现和调用；涉及压缩逻辑时不要只在头部做文章，需覆盖全部已有内容。用户环境已有 openclaw CLI v2026.10.9 可用于验证兼容性。
16: ## 方案决策（2026-11-05 用户确认）
17: 
18: 排查定位到两个关联根因：
19: 1. browser_core.py 的 prompt 按 always_table 构建，工具表在开头且每轮相同，但 msg_condense 的"公共前缀压缩"把工具表每轮压掉，压缩后 TOOL_CONTINUATION_HINT 还指向已变占位符的开头 → CodeArts AI 看不到 cmd/executeCommand 声明所以不调用。
20: 2. msg_condense.py 的 condense 只做公共前缀匹配，CodeArts 每轮 system 规则含动态内容（时间/诊断/todo 状态），前缀提前截止，后面数万字重复历史完全不压缩。
21: 
22: 用户在 A/B/C 三方案中明确回复"a+b"：
23: - **A（已采纳）**：condense() 增加 protect_prefix 参数，prompt 开头协议区（工具表+续传指令）豁免压缩，保证工具表每轮原样出现在最新消息。
24: - **B（已采纳）**：msg_condense 升级为"头部前缀 + 中间块级去重"两级压缩，行级哈希索引+贪心最长匹配，≥800 字符重复块替换为占位符；保持纯函数、不抛异常、条件不满足回退全量发。
25: - **C（用户明确拒绝）**：工具表 JSON 后追加"每工具一行目录"的可读性优化不做。后续不要主动再建议 C，除非用户重新提出。
26: 
27: 验证方式：扩展 test_msg_condense.py（中间块压缩、协议豁免、链式压缩），回归 test_tool_relay.py / test_api_ide.py。

303: @app.post("/v1/chat/completions")
304: async def chat_completions(
305:     request: Request,
306:     body: ChatRequest,
307:     authenticated: bool = Depends(verify_auth)
308: ):
309:     """

313:     1. 请求排队（FIFO）
314:     2. 并发控制

318: 
319:     # ========== 0. 积分耗尽预检（HTTP 层快速失败） ==========

321:     # 上都耗尽时才返回 429，避免客户端（如 OpenClaw）对空流反复
322:     # 重试直到超时；只要还有一个可用组合就交给 worker 正常流转
323:     try:
324:         import browser_core as _bc
325:         _all_sites = list(config_engine.sites.keys())
326:         if _all_sites:

341:                            for m in _cands):

343:                         break
344:                     for m in _cands:
345:                         _until = max(
346:                             _until,
347:                             _bc.CreditStateStore.exhausted_until(s, p, m))
348:                 if _any_avail:
349:                     break
350:             if _insts and not _any_avail:
351:                 _reset = time.strftime(
352:                     '%m-%d %H:%M', time.localtime(_until)) if _until \
353:                     else '每日重置'

358:     except HTTPException:

360:     except Exception:

381: async def _keepalive_stream(
382:     request: Request,
383:     body: ChatRequest,
384:     ctx: RequestContext,
385:     heartbeat_interval: float = 15.0,
386: ):
387:     """网页 AI 首字节等待期（DeepSeek 深度思考可达 90s+）发 SSE 心跳注释行。
388: 
389:     OpenAI 兼容客户端忽略 ': ' 注释行；无心跳时 OpenClaw 等客户端会在
390:     空闲超时后断开并报"无法验证 AI"。生产者任务 + 队列隔离，
391:     心跳超时不会取消下游生成器。"""
392:     queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()
393:     _DONE = object()
394: 
395:     async def _produce():
396:         try:
397:             async for chunk in _usage_aware_stream(request, body, ctx):
398:                 await queue.put(chunk)
399:         except Exception as exc:
400:             await queue.put(exc)
401:         finally:
402:             await queue.put(_DONE)
403: 
404:     task = asyncio.create_task(_produce())
405:     try:
406:         while True:
407:             try:
408:                 item = await asyncio.wait_for(queue.get(),
409:                                               timeout=heartbeat_interval)
410:             except asyncio.TimeoutError:
411:                 yield ": keep-alive\n\n"
412:                 continue
413:             if item is _DONE:
414:                 break
415:             if isinstance(item, Exception):
416:                 raise item
417:             yield item
418:     finally:
419:         if not task.done():
420:             task.cancel()
421:

def match_site_domain(model_id: str, domains: List[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    """model id → 站点域名。精确 > 去点精确 > 子串 > 去点子串；无命中 None。

    去点匹配让 zai/z.a.i 等写法归一到 z.ai。
    复合 id（如 poe/poe.com、poe.com/Assistant）按 "/" 分段逐段尝试，
    任一段命中即返回该站点。"""
    m = (model_id or "").strip().lower()
    if not m:
        return None
    m_flat = m.replace(".", "")
    lowered = [(d, d.lower()) for d in domains if d]
    for _, dl in lowered:
        if m == dl:
            return next(d for d, l in lowered if l == dl)
    for d, dl in lowered:
        if dl.replace(".", "") == m_flat:
            return d
    for d, dl in lowered:
        if m in dl:
            return d
    for d, dl in lowered:
        if m_flat and m_flat in dl.replace(".", ""):
            return d
    # 复合 id 分段匹配（段内不再含 "/"，递归安全）
    if "/" in m:
        for seg in m.split("/"):
            seg = seg.strip()
            if seg and "/" not in seg:
                hit = match_site_domain(seg, domains)
                if hit:
                    return hit
    return None


_MODEL_SLUG_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_\-]{0,63}$")


def extract_model_slug(model_id: str, domain: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    """从 model id 提取站点子模型名（poe 即 bot slug），无则返回 ""（主模型）。

    - "poe.com/Assistant" / "poe/Assistant" → "Assistant"
    - "poe.com" / "poe" / "poe/poe.com" / "web-browser" → ""（主模型）
    """
    m = (model_id or "").strip()
    if not m or "/" not in m or m.lower() in ("web-browser", "default"):
        return ""
    segs = [s.strip() for s in m.split("/") if s.strip()]
    if not segs:
        return ""
    seg = segs[-1]
    if "." in seg:  # 域名样式段不是子模型
        return ""
    if domain:
        dl = (domain or "").lower()
        sl = seg.lower()
        if sl == dl or dl.startswith(sl):
            return ""
    if not _MODEL_SLUG_RE.match(seg):
        return ""
    return seg


def get_site_model_candidates(site_cfg: Optional[dict]) -> List[str]:
    """站点候选模型列表：主模型（entry_url 末段路径）+ FALLBACK_MODELS。

    主模型无路径段时为 ""（站点级）。用于积分耗尽时的同站点换模型。"""
    cfg = site_cfg or {}
    out: List[str] = []
    entry = str(cfg.get("entry_url") or "")
    parts = [p for p in entry.split("/") if p]
    # https://poe.com/Assistant → ['https:', 'poe.com', 'Assistant']，
    # 尾段 Assistant；无路径段（仅域名）→ ""
    if len(parts) > 2:
        out.append(parts[-1])
    for fb in (cfg.get("FALLBACK_MODELS") or []):
        if isinstance(fb, str) and fb.strip():
            out.append(fb.strip())
    seen = set()
    dedup = []
    for m in out:
        if m not in seen:
            seen.add(m)
            dedup.append(m)
    return dedup or [""]

        def _recover_page(site_domain: str = "", exclude_ports=None,
                          credit_models=None):
            """发送失败自恢复：先尝试切换浏览器实例（多账号故障转移，
            site_domain 指定时跳过候选模型积分全部耗尽的账号，
            exclude_ports 跳过本请求已尝试过的账号），切换成功直接用
            新账号重试；无备用实例时刷新会话页重试。"""
            old_port = getattr(browser, "port", None)
            try:
                if browser.switch_instance(exclude_port=old_port,
                                           site_domain=site_domain or None,
                                           exclude_ports=exclude_ports,
                                           credit_models=credit_models):
                    logger.warning(
                        f"请求 [{ctx.request_id}] 已切换到备用账号实例 "
                        f"{getattr(browser, 'instance_name', '?')}:"
                        f"{getattr(browser, 'port', '?')}")
                    time.sleep(2)
                    return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"实例切换失败: {e}")
            try:
                tab = getattr(browser, "tab", None)
                if tab is not None:
                    logger.warning(f"请求 [{ctx.request_id}] 零输出，刷新页面自恢复")
                    tab.refresh()
                    time.sleep(4)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"页面自恢复失败: {e}")
            return False

    def _check_page_status(self, tab) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        result = {"ready": True, "reason": None}
        try:
            url = tab.url or ""
            if not url or url in ("about:blank", "chrome://newtab/"):
                result["ready"] = False
                result["reason"] = "请先打开目标AI网站"
                return result
            error_indicators = ["chrome-error://", "about:neterror"]
            for indicator in error_indicators:
                if indicator in url:
                    result["ready"] = False
                    result["reason"] = "页面加载错误"
                    return result
            title = (tab.title or "").lower()
            warning_keywords = ["404", "not found", "error", "无法访问", "refused"]

5382:                yield from self.execute_workflow(
        """带 per-tab 请求互斥的 execute_workflow。

        CodeArts 会并发发请求（主 agent + 记忆提取等子代理），共享同一个
        网页 tab。不串行的话：A 请求正在生成，B 请求点"新聊天"把 A 的
        会话切走 → A 的后续 tool_result 续传发进陌生对话，上下文全丢
        （实测表现为"任务被拆散""AI 看不到附件"）。加锁后 B 排队等 A
        完整结束（含流式收集）才开始，new_chat 只发生在空闲 tab 上。
        客户端取消时生成器被 close → with 退出 → 锁自动释放。"""
        lock = getattr(self, '_req_lock', None)
        if lock is None:
            lock = threading.Lock()
            self._req_lock = lock
        with lock:
            try:
                yield from self.execute_workflow(
                    messages, stream=stream, tools=tools,

11: 2. **对话压缩不够智能**：现有压缩逻辑（condense/compact，见 msg_condense.py）只压缩头部内容（用户称"g规等头部"），用户要求"存在的都支持压缩"——即所有已有内容都应纳入压缩范围，压缩策略应更智能，而非仅针对头部。
12: 
13: **Why:** 用户的目标是让该代理基础设施与华为 CodeArts CLI 生态正确互操作，且压缩机制直接决定长对话下上下文质量，这是用户主动指出的两大缺陷，不是猜测的需求。
14: 
15: **How to apply:** 后续在此项目工作时，改动涉及工具调用链路（schema 校验、流解析、轮次构建）时优先检查 CodeArts 工具是否能被 AI 正确发现和调用；涉及压缩逻辑时不要只在头部做文章，需覆盖全部已有内容。用户环境已有 openclaw CLI v2026.10.9 可用于验证兼容性。
16: ## 方案决策（2026-11-05 用户确认）
17: 
18: 排查定位到两个关联根因：
19: 1. browser_core.py 的 prompt 按 always_table 构建，工具表在开头且每轮相同，但 msg_condense 的"公共前缀压缩"把工具表每轮压掉，压缩后 TOOL_CONTINUATION_HINT 还指向已变占位符的开头 → CodeArts AI 看不到 cmd/executeCommand 声明所以不调用。
20: 2. msg_condense.py 的 condense 只做公共前缀匹配，CodeArts 每轮 system 规则含动态内容（时间/诊断/todo 状态），前缀提前截止，后面数万字重复历史完全不压缩。
21: 
22: 用户在 A/B/C 三方案中明确回复"a+b"：
23: - **A（已采纳）**：condense() 增加 protect_prefix 参数，prompt 开头协议区（工具表+续传指令）豁免压缩，保证工具表每轮原样出现在最新消息。
24: - **B（已采纳）**：msg_condense 升级为"头部前缀 + 中间块级去重"两级压缩，行级哈希索引+贪心最长匹配，≥800 字符重复块替换为占位符；保持纯函数、不抛异常、条件不满足回退全量发。
25: - **C（用户明确拒绝）**：工具表 JSON 后追加"每工具一行目录"的可读性优化不做。后续不要主动再建议 C，除非用户重新提出。
26: 
27: 验证方式：扩展 test_msg_condense.py（中间块压缩、协议豁免、链式压缩），回归 test_tool_relay.py / test_api_ide.py。
28: ## 实施完成（2026-11-05）
29: 
30: A+B 已实施并验证通过：
31: - msg_condense.py 重写为两级压缩：头部前缀（≥2000）+ 块级去重（`_condense_blocks`，行级精确匹配+贪心延伸，≥800 字符块压缩，`_MIN_LINE_MATCH=24` 短行不参与索引，`_MATCH_BUDGET=1e6` 步超限回退全量）；新增 `protect_prefix` 参数豁免协议区。
32: - browser_core.py 续传轮压缩调用处（约 5546 行）计算协议区长度：`len(tool_protocol(tools))+2 + len(TOOL_CONTINUATION_HINT)+2`（+2 对应 parts 间 `\n\n`）。
33: - 测试：test_msg_condense.py 23 个用例（含块级/豁免/链式/防误吃新内容），全量套件 128 passed。
34: - 端到端模拟 CodeArts 续传轮（build→condense）验证 8 项全过：5894→1614 字符、工具表逐字保留开头、executeCommand 可见、tool_result/时间戳保留、重复历史被占位符替代。
35: - 已知边界：全短行（<24 字符）场景块级索引为空自动回退全量发——设计取舍，真实 IDE prompt 行远长于此。- 端到端验证压缩效果：5894 → 1614 字符（压缩 73%）。

[tool_result name=call_4b2c377253e643628cbc90d3]
---
summary: "WeChat channel setup through the external openclaw-weixin plugin"
read_when:
  - You want to connect OpenClaw to WeChat or Weixin
  - You are installing or troubleshooting the openclaw-weixin channel plugin
  - You need to understand how external channel plugins run beside the Gateway
title: "WeChat"
---

OpenClaw connects to WeChat through Tencent's external
`@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin` channel plugin.

Status: external plugin, maintained by the Tencent Weixin team. Direct chats and
media are supported. Group chats are not advertised by the plugin capability
metadata (it declares direct chats only).

## Naming

- **WeChat** is the user-facing name in these docs.
- **Weixin** is the name used by Tencent's package and by the plugin id.
- `openclaw-weixin` is the OpenClaw channel id (`weixin` and `wechat` work as aliases).
- `@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin` is the npm package.

Use `openclaw-weixin` in CLI commands and config paths.

## How it works

The WeChat code does not live in the OpenClaw core repo. OpenClaw provides the
generic channel plugin contract, and the external plugin provides the
WeChat-specific runtime:

1. `openclaw plugins install` installs `@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin`.
2. The Gateway discovers the plugin manifest and loads the plugin entrypoint.
3. The plugin registers channel id `openclaw-weixin`.
4. `openclaw channels login --channel openclaw-weixin` starts QR login.
5. The plugin stores account credentials under the OpenClaw state directory
   (`~/.openclaw` by default).
6. When the Gateway starts, the plugin starts its Weixin monitor for each
   configured account.
7. Inbound WeChat messages are normalized through the channel contract, routed to
   the selected OpenClaw agent, and sent back through the plugin outbound path.

That separation matters: OpenClaw core stays channel-agnostic. WeChat login,
Tencent iLink API calls, media upload/download, context tokens, and account
monitoring are owned by the external plugin.

## Install

Quick install:

```bash
npx -y @tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin-cli install
```

Manual install:

```bash
openclaw plugins install "@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin"
openclaw plugins enable openclaw-weixin
```

The plugin commands apply changes to a running Gateway. Check the
[application result](/plugins/manage-plugins#apply-changes-and-inspect); start the
Gateway if it is offline.

## Login

Run QR login on the same machine that runs the Gateway:

```bash
openclaw channels login --channel openclaw-weixin
```

Scan the QR code with WeChat on your phone and confirm the login. The plugin saves
the account token locally after a successful scan.

To add another WeChat account, run the same login command again. For multiple
accounts, isolate direct-message sessions by account, channel, and sender:

```bash
openclaw config set session.dmScope per-account-channel-peer
```

## Access control

Version `2.4.8` does not register an OpenClaw pairing adapter or create pairing
requests. The standard pairing list and approve commands cannot establish DM
access for this version. QR login can still allow the user who scanned the code
to chat with the bot.

This version reads a legacy account allowlist JSON file instead of OpenClaw's
SQLite pairing store. When that list is empty, it falls back to the QR scanner's
saved user ID. If neither provides a user ID, its sender check admits any sender
whose message reaches the plugin.

On current OpenClaw, `openclaw doctor --fix` imports legacy approvals into SQLite
and removes the source file. Previously approved secondary
senders can therefore lose access in version `2.4.8`. Revoking an approval in
SQLite does not revoke access granted by the plugin's legacy file or scanner
fallback.

Do not rely on standard pairing to manage or revoke DM access with version
`2.4.8`. If you need pairing enforcement, [temporarily disable the plugin](/channels/wechat#troubleshooting)
until a version with repaired pairing support is available.

For integrations that implement OpenClaw's pairing API, see [Pairing](/channels/pairing).

## Compatibility

The package declares these OpenClaw requirements:

| Plugin version | Declared OpenClaw requirement | npm tag  |
| -------------- | ----------------------------- | -------- |
| `2.4.8`        | `>=2026.5.12`                 | `latest` |
| `1.x`          | `>=2026.1.0 <2026.3.22`       | `legacy` |

Version `2.4.8` declares `>=2026.5.12`, but its startup version guard still checks
`>=2026.3.22`. Passing that guard alone does not satisfy the declared requirement.

If the plugin reports that your OpenClaw version is too old, either update
OpenClaw or install the legacy plugin line:

```bash
openclaw plugins install @tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin@legacy
```

Plugin 2.4.6 imports the retired `openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-runtime` path and
cannot load on OpenClaw 2026.8.1. If startup reports that this subpath is not
exported, update to plugin 2.4.8, which uses the available SDK path:

```bash
openclaw plugins update @tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin@2.4.8
```

## Sidecar process

The WeChat plugin can run helper work beside the Gateway while it monitors the
Tencent iLink API. In [issue #68451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/68451), that helper path exposed a bug in OpenClaw's
generic stale-Gateway cleanup: a child process could try to clean up the parent
Gateway process, causing restart loops under process managers such as systemd.

Current OpenClaw startup cleanup excludes the current process and its ancestors,
so a channel helper cannot kill the Gateway that launched it. This fix is
generic; it is not a WeChat-specific path in core.

## Troubleshooting

Check install and status:

```bash
openclaw plugins list
openclaw channels status --probe
openclaw --version
```

If the channel shows as installed but does not connect, enable it and inspect the
running plugin:

```bash
openclaw plugins enable openclaw-weixin
openclaw plugins inspect openclaw-weixin --runtime --json
```

If the Gateway restarts repeatedly after enabling WeChat, update both OpenClaw and
the plugin:

```bash
npm view @tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin version
openclaw plugins install "@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin" --force
openclaw gateway restart
```

If startup reports that the installed plugin package `requires compiled runtime
output for TypeScript entry`, the npm package was published without the compiled
JavaScript runtime files OpenClaw needs. Update/reinstall after the plugin
publisher ships a fixed package, or temporarily disable/uninstall the plugin.

Temporary disable:

```bash
openclaw plugins disable openclaw-weixin
```

- Pairing: [Pairing](/channels/pairing)
- Channel routing: [Channel routing](/channels/channel-routing)

- Channel plugin SDK: [Channel Plugin SDK](/plugins/sdk-channel-plugins)
- External package: [@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin)


[tool_result name=prt_1a84ba01c001JnUeBaDfdOirvw]
---
summary: "WeChat channel setup through the external openclaw-weixin plugin"
read_when:
  - You want to connect OpenClaw to WeChat or Weixin
  - You are installing or troubleshooting the openclaw-weixin channel plugin
  - You need to understand how external channel plugins run beside the Gateway
title: "WeChat"
---

OpenClaw connects to WeChat through Tencent's external
`@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin` channel plugin.

Status: external plugin, maintained by the Tencent Weixin team. Direct chats and
media are supported. Group chats are not advertised by the plugin capability
metadata (it declares direct chats only).

## Naming

- **WeChat** is the user-facing name in these docs.
- **Weixin** is the name used by Tencent's package and by the plugin id.
- `openclaw-weixin` is the OpenClaw channel id (`weixin` and `wechat` work as aliases).
- `@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin` is the npm package.

Use `openclaw-weixin` in CLI commands and config paths.

## How it works

The WeChat code does not live in the OpenClaw core repo. OpenClaw provides the
generic channel plugin contract, and the external plugin provides the
WeChat-specific runtime:

1. `openclaw plugins install` installs `@tencent-weixin/openclaw-weixin`.
2. The Gateway discovers the plugin manifest and loads the plugin entrypoint.
3. The plugin registers channel id `openclaw-weixin`.
4. `openclaw channels login --channel openclaw-weixin` starts QR login.
5. The plugin stores account credentials under the OpenClaw state directory
   (`~/.openclaw` by default).
6. When the Gateway starts, the plugin starts its Weixin monitor for each
   configured account.
7. Inbound WeChat messages are normalized through the channel contract, routed to
   the selected OpenClaw agent, and sent back through the plugin outbound path.

That separation matters: OpenClaw core stays channel-agnostic. WeChat login,
Tencent iLink API calls, media upload/download, context tokens, and account
monitoring are owned by the external plugin.


用户确认）\r\n\r\n排查定位到两个关联根因：\r\n1. browser_core.py 的 prompt 按 always_table 构建，工具表在开头且每轮相同，但 msg_condense 的"公共前缀压缩"把工具表每轮压掉，压缩后 TOOL_CONTINUATION_HINT 还指向已变占位符的开头 → CodeArts AI 看不到 cmd/executeCommand 声明所以不调用。\r\n2. msg_condense.py 的 condense 只做公共前缀匹配，CodeArts 每轮 system 规则含动态内容（时间/诊断/todo 状态），前缀提前截止，后面数万字重复历史完全不压缩。\r\n\r\n用户在 A/B/C 三方案中明确回复"a+b"：\r\n- **A（已采纳）**：condense() 增加 protect_prefix 参数，prompt 开头协议区（工具表+续传指令）豁免压缩，保证工具表每轮原样出现在最新消息。\r\n- **B（已采纳）**：msg_condense 升级为"头部前缀 + 中间块级去重"两级压缩，行级哈希索引+贪心最长匹配，≥800 字符重复块替换为占位符；保持纯函数、不抛异常、条件不满足回退全量发。\r\n- **C（用户明确拒绝）**：工具表 JSON 后追加"每工具一行目录"的可读性优化不做。后续不要主动再建议 C，除非用户重新提出。\r\n\r\n验证方式：扩展 test_msg_condense.py（中间块压缩、协议豁免、链式压缩），回归 test_tool_relay.py / test_api_ide.py。\r\n## 实施完成（2026-11-05）\r\n\r\nA+B 已实施并验证通过：\r\n-
